# Dhātupāṭha — Devanāgarī Decomposition

> Source: `data/dhatupatha.csv` (github.com/sanskrit/vyakarana)
>
> Anubandhas stripped per *Aṣṭādhyāyī* 1.3.2 (final short -a/-i/-u after consonant), 1.3.3 (trailing single-consonant anubandhas Y/N/l/S/z/w/q after a vowel), and 1.3.5 (initial *ñi*/*ṭu*/*ḍu*).
>
> Total entries (post-stripping): **2168** across the ten gaṇāḥ.
>
> Generated by `scripts/decompose_dhatupatha.py`.

---

Each row shows the dhātu in standard Devanāgarī (with inherent /a/, vowel diacritics, and halants where needed) on the left of the `=`, followed by its varṇa-level decomposition (each consonant with its halant ् and each vowel in its independent form) on the right. Pattern and particle count for each in parentheses.

---

- **व्रण्** = व् + र् + अ + ण्  *(CCVC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `vraRa~`)*
- **वर्ण्** = व् + अ + र् + ण्  *(CVCC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `varRa~`)*
- **पर्ण्** = प् + अ + र् + ण्  *(CVCC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `parRa~`)*
- **विष्क्** = व् + इ + ष् + क्  *(CVCC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `vizka~`)*
- **क्षप्** = क् + ष् + अ + प्  *(CCVC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `kzapa~`)*
- **वस्** = व् + अ + स्  *(CVC, 3 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `vasa~`)*
- **तुत्थ्** = त् + उ + त् + थ्  *(CVCC, 4 part., 1 akṣ.; SLP1 `tutTa~`)*
